Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a core algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 221. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 898/3.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by core generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 221, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 898. QED.
